# Research Ledger

This file records the current canonical status of research claims in `reimann_scope`.

It is not a historical narrative. Git history preserves superseded paths.

Every proof-facing claim should be classified as one of:

- **PROVED**
- **FALSE**
- **CIRCULAR**
- **KNOWN INSUFFICIENT**
- **OPEN**
- **RESEARCH REQUIREMENT**
- **DESIGN DEFINITION**

No agent should promote a claim without changing its classification here and documenting the reason.

---

# 1. Central framework

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-001 | `Transcendental continuation` is the project-defined extension \(\mathcal Z_\tau(s,K)=\zeta(\tau^{-K}s)\) across canonical real algebraic grades \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} = \overline{\mathbb Q} \cap \mathbb R\). | **DESIGN DEFINITION** | This names the formal framework used by the repo. |
| TC-002 | \(K=0\) recovers ordinary analytically continued zeta: \(\mathcal Z_\tau(s,0)=\zeta(s)\). | **PROVED** | Direct substitution. |
| TC-003 | Integer \(K \in \mathbb Z\) gives the canonical discrete skeleton \(\tau^K\); canonical domain is \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\). | **DESIGN DEFINITION** | Integer skeleton inside algebraic domain. |
| TC-004 | Rational grades provide root refinements; continuous real coordinate \(k \in \mathbb R\) provides ambient embedding and scale flow; canonical TC domain is \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\). | **PROVED** | \(\tau^{\mathbb Z} \subset \tau^{\mathbb Q} \subset \tau^{\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}} \subset \tau^{\mathbb R} = \mathbb R_{>0}\). |
| TC-005 | Transcendental continuation is standard established terminology in analytic number theory. | **FALSE** | It is a project-defined term. |
| TC-006 | The framework itself proves RH. | **FALSE** | It supplies an extended geometry and candidate constraints only. |
| TC-007 | Informational redundancy vs constraint: "No new information \(\not\Rightarrow\) no new mathematical constraint". Redundancy is the construction; do not quotient it away. | **DESIGN DEFINITION** | Maximum redundancy across grades to expose joint compatibility conditions. |
| TC-008 | Pure Pullback Null Model: Generic meromorphic function \(f\) under \(F_K(s) = f(\tau^{-K}s)\) satisfies all pullback identities with zero radial rigidity on \(\delta\). | **PROVED / KNOWN INSUFFICIENT** | Exact coordinate transport alone imposes zero restriction on zero locations. |

# 2. Graded arithmetic grids and universe claims

Define for every algebraic grade \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\):

\[
L_K = \{n\tau^K : n \in \pm\mathbb N\}, \qquad \mathfrak L_\tau = \bigcup_{K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}} L_K.
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| L-001 | Every \(L_K\) has cardinality \(\aleph_0\). | **PROVED** | Scaling is a bijection from \(\mathbb Z \setminus \{0\}\). |
| L-002 | Distinct rational-difference grids satisfy \(L_J\cap L_K=\emptyset\) for all \(K - J \in \mathbb Q \setminus \{0\}\). | **PROVED** | Nonzero intersection would make \(\tau^p \in \mathbb Q\) for \(p \ne 0 \in \mathbb Z\), contradicting transcendence of \(\tau\). |
| L-002B | Distinct irrational-algebraic-difference grids satisfy \(L_J\cap L_K=\emptyset\) for \(K - J \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \setminus \mathbb Q\). | **OPEN_TAU_ALGEBRAIC_EXPONENT_ARITHMETIC** | Gelfond-Schneider requires algebraic base and does not apply to \(\tau\); control counterexample \(b = 2^{1/\sqrt{2}}\) has \(b^{\sqrt{2}} = 2 \in \mathbb Q\). |
| L-003 | Total grid universe \(\mathfrak L_\tau\) has cardinality \(\aleph_0\). | **PROVED** | Countable union of countable sets. |
| L-004 | Rational-difference algebraic grids are scale-isomorphic, equipotent, and arithmetically disjoint. | **PROVED / TERMINOLOGY** | Summarizes L-001 through L-003 for rational grade differences. |
| L-005 | Distinct arbitrary real-grade lattices are always noncoincident. | **FALSE** | Example \(k=\log_\tau 2\) gives \(L_k=2\mathbb Z\), overlapping \(L_0 = \mathbb Z\). |
| L-006 | The coordinate spaces of distinct grades are non-isomorphic. | **FALSE** | Multiplication by the scale ratio gives an invertible real map. |
| L-007 | Parallel construction means one line must be finitely traversed to reach another. | **FALSE** | The framework constructs grades by making grade part of the coordinate. |
| L-008 | Exact symbolic grade identity and finite numerical realization are the same thing. | **FALSE** | The repo explicitly separates them. |
| L-009 | TC Coverage Conjecture: \(\mathfrak L_\tau \stackrel{?}{=} \mathbb R^\times\). | **PROJECT CONJECTURE / NOT A THEOREM** | Literal set equality fails in ZFC (\(\aleph_0 < 2^{\aleph_0}\)); dense closure \(\overline{\mathfrak L_\tau} = \mathbb R\); must never be assumed as an unproved lemma. |
| L-010 | Scale generator algebraic multiples \(a\tau\) (\(a \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}^\times\)) generate identical integer grid universes: \(L_{K, a\tau} = L_{K, \tau}\). | **FALSE / OPEN COMPARISON** | \(na^K\) is algebraic, not necessarily an integer in \(\pm\mathbb N\); coefficient domains differ. |
| L-013 | Analytic TC dilation \(A_K[\zeta](s) = \zeta(\tau^{-K}s)\) and arithmetic grid dilation \(D_K[\zeta](s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s)\) are fundamentally distinct operations. Analytic scaling induces base power \(n \mapsto n^{\tau^{-K}}\) (log-space dilation \(x \mapsto \tau^{-K}x\)); arithmetic grid scaling induces linear dilation \(n \mapsto \tau^Kn\) (log-space translation \(x \mapsto x + K\log\tau\)). Implicit identification is classified as `CONFLATED_ANALYTIC_AND_GRID_DILATION`. | **PROVED / DISTINCTION ESTABLISHED** | Derived symbolically and validated in `tests/test_tc_analytic_vs_arithmetic_intertwining.py` and `docs/reviews/TC_ANALYTIC_ARITHMETIC_INTERTWINING.md`. |
| L-014 | Analytic dilation \(A_K\) and arithmetic grid dilation \(D_J\) do not commute for \(J \ne 0, K \ne 0\). They satisfy the exact semidirect relation \(A_K D_J = D_{J\tau^{-K}} A_K\). In log-space, translations \(S_K(x) = x + K\log\tau\) and dilations \(T_J(x) = \tau^{-J}x\) generate \(\operatorname{Aff}_+(\mathbb R)\) with affine commutator \([S_K, T_J](x) = (1 - \tau^{-J})K\log\tau\). | **PROVED** | Symbolic identity \(\tau^{-J\tau^{-K}s}\zeta(\tau^{-K}s)\) verified; group homomorphism \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \to \operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb R)\). |
| L-015 | Evaluating the normalized multiplier ratio of \(A_K D_J\) and \(D_J A_K\) along the zero worldline \(s = \tau^K\rho\) produces normalized modulus \(\tau^{J(\tau^K-1)\delta}\). Symmetrizing with respect to reflection yields the exact reflection defect \(B_\rho(K_{\mathrm{eff}}) = 4\sinh^2(K_{\mathrm{eff}}\delta\log\tau/2)\ge 0\) with \(K_{\mathrm{eff}} = J(\tau^K-1)\), vanishing if and only if \(\delta=0\). | **PROVED IDENTITY** | Derived in `docs/reviews/TC_ANALYTIC_ARITHMETIC_INTERTWINING.md` and verified in unit tests. |
| L-016 | Graded arithmetic monoid \(\mathcal M_\tau = \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \times \mathbb Z_{\ne 0}\) is multiplicatively closed under \((K, n) \star (J, m) = (K+J, nm)\) with realization homomorphism \(\Phi_\tau(K, n) = n\tau^K\) into \((\mathbb R^\times, \times)\). Unit group is \(U(\mathcal M_\tau) \cong \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \times \mathbb Z/2\mathbb Z\), with grade units \(u_K = (K, 1)\). Every element has unique canonical factorization \(u_K \star (0, \operatorname{sgn} n) \star \prod_p (0, p)^{v_p(|n|)}\); TC grades do NOT create new primes. Quotient \(\mathcal M_\tau / U_{\mathrm{grade}} \cong \mathbb Z_{\ne 0}\) recovers ordinary integer arithmetic. Fixed-grade Dirichlet series \(D_K[\zeta](s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s) = \chi_s(u_K)\zeta(s)\) is a global grade-unit twist of the universal native Euler product \(\tau^{-Ks}\prod_p(1-p^{-s})^{-1}\). | **PROVED / EXACT ALGEBRA** | Formalized in `formal/RiemannScope/GradedMonoid.lean`, verified in `tests/test_tc_graded_arithmetic_closure.py`, documented in `docs/reviews/TC_GRADED_ARITHMETIC_CLOSURE.md`. |
| L-017 | Rational-Grade Escape Theorem: For all \(K \in \mathbb Q \setminus \{0\}\) and \(J \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \setminus \{0\}\), \(J\tau^{-K} \notin \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) by Lindemann (1882) transcendence of \(\pi\). Under analytic dilation, \((J, n) \mapsto (J\tau^{-K}, n^{\tau^{-K}})\) simultaneously escapes both the algebraic grade domain \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) and the integer lattice \(\mathbb Z\) (elementary nonintegrality witness: \(1 < 2^{\tau^{-K}} < 2\) for \(K > 0\); general transcendence status is OPEN; counter-control \(\alpha = \log_2 3\) is transcendental yet \(2^\alpha = 3 \in \mathbb Z\)). Generic-base control: for algebraic base \(b=2\), rational grades preserve algebraic closure (\(J b^{-K} \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\)); grade escape is specific to transcendental bases. | **PROVED THEOREM** | Exact symbolic proof via Lindemann; verified in `tests/test_tc_graded_arithmetic_closure.py`. |
| L-018 | Governing Decision Point (Fork B Resolution): Algebraic-grade coordinate closure is an imposed convention on the indexing set, not an intrinsic requirement of \(\zeta\) or TC. The escape \(J\tau^{-K} \notin \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) holds unconditionally and identically whether RH is true or false (\(\delta\)-independent). Analytic dilation naturally escapes into the ambient completion \(\mathcal M_\tau^{\mathrm{ambient}} = \Gamma_\tau \times \exp(\Lambda_\tau) \subset \mathbb R \times \mathbb R_{>0}\), where \(\Gamma_\tau = \operatorname{span}_{\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}}\{\tau^K : K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\}\) is a countable algebraic-transcendental ring and \(\Lambda_\tau = \operatorname{span}_{\mathbb Z}\{\tau^K \log p : K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}, p \in \mathcal P\}\). Affine group generated on algebraic grades \(G_{\mathrm{TC}} = \mathcal T_{\mathrm{closure}} \rtimes \tau^{\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}}\) is a countable dense proper subgroup of \(\operatorname{Aff}_+(\mathbb R)\). | **PROVED FORK B RESOLUTION** | Derived in `docs/reviews/TC_GRADED_ARITHMETIC_CLOSURE.md` and verified in unit tests. |
| L-019 | Zero-Induced Grade-Unit Characters and TC Unitarity Reformulation of RH: For nontrivial zero \(\rho = 1/2 + \delta + i\gamma\), \(\eta_\rho(K) = \tau^{-K(\rho - 1/2)}\) is a multiplicative group character on the integer skeleton \(K \in \mathbb Z\) with modulus \(|\eta_\rho(K)| = \tau^{-K\delta}\). RH is strictly equivalent to unitarity of all zero characters: \(\eta_\rho \text{ unitary on } \mathbb Z \iff \delta = 0\). The spectral detector \(B_\rho(K) = |\eta_\rho(K)| + |\eta_\rho(K)|^{-1} - 2 = 4\sinh^2(K\delta\log\tau/2)\) is the exact non-unitarity defect of the grade character. Multiplicative dilation is unitary on \(L^2(\mathbb R_{>0}, dx/x)\); on \(L^2(\mathbb R_{>0}, dx)\), normalized dilation \((U_K f)(x) = \tau^{K/2} f(\tau^K x)\) yields Mellin multiplier \(\tau^{-K(s - 1/2)} = \eta_s(K)\), deriving the \(1/2\) centering from the Mellin-Plancherel isometry line. Dual equals adjoint on \(\mathbb Z\) iff \(\delta = 0\). Positive-definite sequences on \(\mathbb Z\) admit only unitary characters in their Herglotz spectral measure on \(\mathbb T\); linking arithmetic grade correlations to a pure zero spectrum is classified as `EQUIVALENT_REFORMULATION_OF_WEIL`. | **PROVED REFORMULATION & CHARACTER DEFECT IDENTITY** | Formalized in Lean 4 (`formal/RiemannScope/GradeCharacter.lean`), verified in `tests/test_tc_grade_character_unitarity.py`, documented in `docs/reviews/TC_GRADE_CHARACTER_UNITARITY.md` and `data/tc_grade_character_unitarity.json`. |
| L-020 | Suzuki Localized Weil Positivity and Scale Covariance: Suzuki (2026 [W10]) proves unconditional local positivity \(\lambda_1(R) > 0\) for \(R < R_0\), but global screw-function positivity is `RH_EQUIVALENT` (Suzuki 2023 [W2]). Under analytic dilation \(S_K\), discrete prime stations \(\log p\) break operator similarity: \(S_K^* A_{\tau^{-K} R} S_K \ne A_R\) (`NO_NATURAL_INTERTWINER`). Dilation rescales the interval and operator simultaneously without controlling new physical domain (`PURE_COORDINATE_RESCALING_NO_GAIN`). Log-translation preserves form values for single vectors (\(Q_W(T_h v) = Q_W(v)\)), but cross-grade linear combinations require full Weil positivity (\(C_h(0) = Q_W(h) \ge 0\)). TC grade covariance cannot propagate local positivity to global scales without assuming an RH-equivalent premise. | **PROVED_PURE_RESCALING** | Derived in `docs/reviews/TC_SUZUKI_LOCALIZED_GRADE_COVARIANCE.md` and verified in `tests/test_tc_suzuki_grade_covariance.py`. |
| L-021 | Two-Pairing Firewall, Exceptional Exponent Rigidity, and Rational-Grade Algebraic Separation: Reflected pairing \(Q_W\) is unconditionally shift-invariant but positivity is `RH_EQUIVALENT`; ordinary Gram pairing \(Q_+\) is unconditionally positive but shift-sensitivity off-line breaks invariance (\(e^{-2\delta h}\)). For exceptional exponents \(S_\tau = \{\alpha \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} : \tau^\alpha \in \overline{\mathbb Q}\}\), \(S_\tau \cap \mathbb Q = \{0\}\) and Gelfond–Schneider proves \(\dim_{\mathbb Q} S_\tau \le 1\) (`PROVED_FROM_GELFOND_SCHNEIDER`). For algebraic coefficients, \(m\tau^K = n\tau^J \iff K = J \text{ and } m = n\) for all \(m, n \in \overline{\mathbb Q}^\times, K, J \in \mathbb Q\) (`PROVED_EXACT`). Audit of current TC mechanisms confirms no second independent exponent direction in \(S_\tau\) (`TWO_DIRECTION_COLLISION_BRIDGE_OPEN`). | **EXCEPTIONAL EXPONENTS COLLINEAR; ALGEBRAIC SEPARATION PROVED; FIREWALL ENFORCED** | Formalized in Lean 4 (`formal/RiemannScope/TranscendenceRigidity.lean`), verified in `tests/test_tc_suzuki_grade_covariance.py`. |

| N-002 | A finite positional numerical representation exhausts the full real value of nonzero \(\tau^K\). | **FALSE** | Its positional expansion is nonterminating. |

# 4. Zero worldlines and radial leaves

For a native zero

\[
\rho=\frac12+\delta+i\gamma,
\]

define for \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\):

\[
s_\rho(K)=\tau^K\rho
\]

and

\[
R_\tau(s,K)=\tau^{-K}\Re(s)-\frac12.
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| W-001 | \(s_\rho(K)\) is a zero of \(\mathcal Z_\tau(\cdot,K)\) for all \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\). | **PROVED** | \(\zeta(\tau^{-K}\tau^K\rho)=\zeta(\rho)=0\). |
| W-002 | The critical line becomes \(\Re(s)=\tau^K/2\). | **PROVED** | Origin-dilation image. |
| W-003 | \(R_\tau(s_\rho(K),K)=\delta\) along every zero worldline across \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\). | **PROVED** | Exact cancellation. |
| W-004 | Transcendental continuation preserves normalized radial class across all algebraic grades. | **PROVED** | W-003. |
| W-005 | Compression forces an off-line worldline to intersect the critical surface. | **FALSE** | Absolute defect shrinks but normalized \(\delta\) remains nonzero. |
| W-006 | The existence of multiple mathematical radial leaves is contradictory. | **FALSE** | The ambient geometry permits them. |
| W-007 | RH is equivalent to all actual nontrivial zero worldlines occupying \(R_\tau=0\). | **PROVED REFORMULATION** | Equivalent to \(\Re(\rho)=1/2\). |

# 5. Central contradiction programme

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-001 | If RH is false, the actual nontrivial spectrum contains an occupied \(R_\tau\neq0\) leaf in addition to established \(R_\tau=0\) zeros. | **PROVED CONDITIONAL STRUCTURE** | Definition of a counterexample plus known on-line zeros. |
| R-002 | A counterexample generates a bilateral transcendental radial defect worldline across \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\). | **PROVED** | Exact worldline and grade formulas. |
| R-003 | The complete algebraic grade family may impose a global coherence law not reducible to coordinate covariance. | **OPEN** | Central discovery target. |
| R-004 | Such a coherence law forces only one occupied radial leaf. | **OPEN** | Missing radial-rigidity theorem. |
| R-005 | `Transcendental Coherence -> Transcendental Radial Rigidity` is the central missing implication. | **OPEN / CURRENT TARGET** | Core proof programme. |
| R-006 | Global participation of all zeros in one xi/zeta object automatically forces one radial leaf. | **FALSE AS AN INFERENCE** | Global dependence does not by itself equal radial rigidity. |
| R-007 | An off-line zero is impossible because there is literally no geometric space left between grade lines. | **FALSE / UNSUPPORTED** | Countable/dense families do not fill the continuum in that sense. |
| R-008 | The train-line intuition is better represented as intersection of exact grade constraints than literal intersection of points. | **CURRENT FORMULATION** | Mathematically stronger and avoids the space-filling error. |
| R-009 | Earliest Missing Implication (True Open Theorem): Construct a zero-independent, zeta-intrinsic functional \(\mathscr A_K\) such that \(\mathscr A_K - \mathscr A_0 = \sum w_\rho B_\rho(K)\) with \(w_\rho > 0\). | **RESEARCH REQUIREMENT / OPEN** | Same-referent invariance \(\mathscr A_K = \mathscr A_0\) forces \(\sum w_\rho B_\rho(K) = 0 \implies \delta_\rho = 0 \;\forall \rho \implies \text{RH}\). Downstream machinery is conditional on this construction. |
| R-010 | Minimal Spectral Detector: \(B_\rho(K) = 4\sinh^2(K\delta\log\tau / 2) \ge 0\), and \(B_\rho(K) = 0 \iff \delta = 0\) for every nonzero algebraic grade \(K \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \setminus \{0\}\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact finite-grade hyperbolic identity; requires no numerical estimates or continuous derivatives. |
| R-011 | Generic-Base Detector Control: \(B_{\rho,b}(K) = 4\sinh^2(K\delta\log b / 2) \ge 0\) and \(= 0 \iff \delta = 0\) for any base \(b > 1\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Proves hyperbolic positivity is generic and NOT \(\tau\)-specific; constraint mechanism must reside in \(\mathscr A_K\). |
| R-012 | Common-Grade Shift Translation: \(G_\rho(K, J) = \chi_\rho(K)\overline{\chi_\rho(J)} \implies G_\rho(K+A, J+A) = \tau^{2A\delta} G_\rho(K, J)\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Common-grade translation invariance \(G(K+A, J+A) = G(K, J)\) forces \(\tau^{2A\delta} = 1 \implies \delta = 0\). |

q_\rho=\tau^{\rho-\frac12},
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |

# 8. Exact symmetry-complete defect

For

\[
\rho_\pm=\frac12\pm\delta+i\gamma,
\qquad
\rho_0=\frac12+i\gamma,
\]

define

\[
D_K=q_+^K+q_-^K-2q_0^K.
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification |
|---|---|---|
| D-001 | \(D_K=2e^{iK\gamma\log\tau}[\cosh(K\delta\log\tau)-1]\). | **PROVED** |
| D-002 | \(|D_K|=4\sinh^2(K\delta\log\tau/2)\). | **PROVED** |
| D-003 | The defect is even in \(\delta\). | **PROVED** |
| D-004 | Near \(K\delta=0\), \(|D_K|\sim(K\delta\log\tau)^2\). | **PROVED** |
| D-005 | The symmetry-complete defect proves RH. | **KNOWN INSUFFICIENT** |

# 9. Spectrum-wide grade identity

For \(K>0\),

\[
\sum_\rho\frac{q_\rho^K}{\rho}
=
\tau^{-K/2}
\left[
\tau^K-\psi(\tau^K)-\log\tau
-\frac12\log(1-\tau^{-2K})
\right].
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification |
|---|---|---|
| A-001 | A genuine global arithmetic grade identity exists. | **PROVED** |
| A-002 | The identity proves grade language is connected to the complete zero sum. | **PROVED** |
| A-003 | The displayed arithmetic realization is bilateral in \(K\). | **FALSE / NOT ESTABLISHED** |
| A-004 | Existence of the identity alone forces one radial class. | **KNOWN INSUFFICIENT** |
| A-005 | Bounding the identity at RH scale gives a non-circular intermediate proof. | **CIRCULAR / RH-STRENGTH** |

# 11. Cross-height coherence

For verified simple zeros

\[
\rho_n=\frac12+i\gamma_n,
\]

define a baseline local scale

\[
\Delta_n
=
\frac{\tau}{\log(\gamma_n/\tau)}
\]

and normalized path

\[
P_n(u)
=
\frac{
\zeta(\frac12+i(\gamma_n+\Delta_nu))
}{
i\Delta_n\zeta'(\rho_n)
}.
\]

| ID | Statement | Classification |
|---|---|---|
| X-001 | \(P_n(0)=0\) and \(P_n'(0)=1\). | **PROVED for simple zeros** |
| X-002 | This removes selected zero location, local mean scale, first-order magnitude, and first-order orientation. | **PROVED** |
| X-003 | All \(P_n\) converge to one universal path. | **OPEN** |
| X-004 | Some simpler invariant of the \(P_n\) is height-independent. | **OPEN** |
| X-005 | Cross-height normalization is the definition of transcendental continuation. | **FALSE** |
| X-006 | Cross-height comparison is one instrument for discovering a zeta-specific coherence law. | **CURRENT RESEARCH ROLE** |

# 17. Current research stop condition

The next work should not broaden into additional RH machinery until the following question is tested:

\[
\boxed{
\text{Do the exact parallel grade constraints of transcendental continuation}
\text{ reveal a nontrivial zeta-specific coherence law that forbids}
\text{ simultaneous occupancy of multiple radial leaves?}
}
\]

If no such law survives the declared controls, the current proof programme should be revised or killed.

If a simple law survives, broad computation should stop until that law is algebraically derived or falsified.

| EF-003 | Grade-\(K\) test function \(h_{K,j}(t) = H_j(a_K t)\) has Fourier transform \(\widehat h_{K,j}(x) = a_K^{-1} \widehat H_j(a_K^{-1} x)\). | **PROVED** | Change of variables in Fourier integral. |
| EF-004 | Prime frequencies in grade \(K\) scale as \(a_K^{-1} \log n = \tau^{-K} \log n\). | **PROVED** | Follows from EF-003. |
| EF-005 | \(\mathcal C_{K,j}[H] \equiv \mathcal C_0[H(a_K \cdot)]\). | **PROVED** | Direct coordinate pullback identity. |
| EF-006 | The grade-\(K\) constraint family is coordinate-redundant with the expanded \(K=0\) native basis \(\{ H_j(a_K \cdot) \}\). | **PROVED / REDUNDANT** | The span of grade-\(K\) constraints is identical to scaled native constraints (exact theoretical classification: `coordinate_redundant`; relative to an unexpanded finite basis: `finite_basis_enrichment_only`). |
| EF-007 | Isolated zero-divisor modifications produce non-zero finite divisor defects \(\Delta \mathcal C_{K,j} = \langle\Delta\mathcal D, h_{K,j}\rangle\) while holding \(\mathcal A_\zeta\) fixed. | **PROVED** | Exact algebraic cancellation of unchanged arithmetic, pole, and gamma terms. |
| EF-008 | Radial quartet substitution decomposes into height-merging baseline \(\Delta\mathcal C^{\mathrm{merge}}\) and pure radial defect \(\Delta\mathcal C^{\mathrm{radial}}(\delta)\), with \(\Delta\mathcal C^{\mathrm{radial}}(0)=0\). | **PROVED** | Exact algebraic decomposition of four-zero symmetric sum. |
| EF-009 | Defect exposure under fixed arithmetic data proves radial rigidity of actual zeta zeros. | **FALSE INFERENCE** | Modifying zero divisor with fixed \(\mathcal A\) is a local sensitivity diagnostic of a synthetic object; it does not model an alternative zeta function or prove global non-compensation. |
| EF-010 | Independent Fourier numerical quadrature in native variable \(u = a_K t\) over compact effective support achieves certified error bound \(\le 10^{-45}\) without frequency aliasing. | **PROVED / IMPLEMENTED** | Panel-subdivided tanh-sinh quadrature control verified across all 90 \((K, j, x)\) channels. |
| EF-011 | Prospective cross-grade joint arithmetic constraints without test function dilation are mathematically distinct from \(\mathcal C_{K,j}\) and categorized as open candidates. | **CLASSIFICATION RULE** | Prevents conflating coordinate dilation with non-trivial joint arithmetic restrictions. |

# 20. Radial-Defect Quotient, limiting invariant \(L_Q\), and relative Fredholm formulation

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| RDQ-001 | Baseline completed reference function \(\Xi^\flat(z) = \prod_{\gamma>0}(1 + z^2/\gamma^2)^{m_\gamma}\) matches \(\Xi(z)\) for all critical-line zeros (\(\delta=0\)), with \(m_\gamma = m_{0,\gamma} + 2\sum_j n_{j,\gamma}\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact factorization cancellation for \(\delta=0\) pairs \(\pm i\gamma\) in paired Hadamard product. |
| RDQ-002 | Radial-Defect Quotient \(Q(z) = \frac{\Xi(z)}{\Xi(0)\Xi^\flat(z)}\) decomposes on off-line quartets into real-axis factor \(q_{\delta,\gamma}(x) = \frac{\gamma^4[(x^2+\gamma^2-\delta^2)^2+4\delta^2\gamma^2]}{(\gamma^2+\delta^2)^2(x^2+\gamma^2)^2}\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact algebraic normalization of 4-zero product divided by baseline factor. |
| RDQ-003 | Real-axis factor satisfies \(0 < q_{\delta,\gamma}(x) \le 1\) for all \(x \in \mathbb R\), with exact factorization \(1 - q_{\delta,\gamma}(x) = \frac{\delta^2 x^2 [(\delta^2+2\gamma^2)x^2 + 2\gamma^2(\delta^2+3\gamma^2)]}{(\delta^2+\gamma^2)^2(x^2+\gamma^2)^2} \ge 0\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact rational difference factorization; equality iff \(x=0\) (for \(\delta \ne 0\)) and \(q_{0,\gamma}(x) \equiv 1\). |
| RDQ-004 | Unique minimum in \(u = x^2\) at \(u_* = \delta^2 + 3\gamma^2\), corresponding to two real minimizers \(x = \pm\sqrt{\delta^2 + 3\gamma^2}\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact derivative root analysis of rational function. |
| RDQ-005 | For \(r = \delta^2/\gamma^2\), the minimum value is \(q_{\min} = \frac{4}{(1+r)^2(4+r)}\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Direct evaluation at \(x = \pm\sqrt{\delta^2+3\gamma^2}\). |
| RDQ-006 | Exact uniform estimate \(\sup_x \|\log q_{\delta,\gamma}(x)\| = 2\log(1+r) + \log(1+r/4) \le \frac{9}{4}r\) supplies domination for infinite product/limit interchange. | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact calculus extremum of log-ratio and \(\sum n_j \delta_j^2/\gamma_j^2 < \infty\). |
| RDQ-007 | Limiting invariant \(L_Q = \lim_{x\to\infty} Q(x) = \prod_{j} (\frac{\gamma_j^2}{\gamma_j^2+\delta_j^2})^{2n_j}\) satisfies \(0 < L_Q \le 1\), with \(L_Q = 1 \iff \mathrm{RH}\). | **PROVED SPECTRAL EQUIVALENCE** | Asymptotic limit of uniform convergent product; exact spectral criterion. |
| RDQ-008 | For \(H(z) = \log z\), projection-subtracted quartet response is \(2\Re\log(\delta+i\gamma) - 2\Re\log(i\gamma) = \log(1+\delta^2/\gamma^2) = d(\delta,\gamma)\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact complex logarithm real-part identity. |
| RDQ-009 | The defect \(-\log L_Q = 2\sum n_j d(\delta_j,\gamma_j)\) lies inside the projection-subtracted EF-013 construction; its bridge failure is due to test-class inadmissibility, the projected-divisor problem, and unproved finite compensation. | **METHODOLOGICAL STATUS** | Rigorous attribution of EF-013 structural content. |
| RDQ-010 | Positive diagonal spectral operator \(\mathcal R e_\lambda = \frac{\delta_\lambda^2}{\gamma_\lambda^2} e_\lambda\) on \(\ell^2(\Lambda^+)\) is trace-class with \(\operatorname{Tr}\mathcal R = \sum \frac{\delta^2}{\gamma^2} < \infty\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Convergence follows from \(\sum \gamma^{-2} < \infty\) and \(|\delta| < 1/2\). |
| RDQ-011 | Relative Fredholm determinant satisfies \(\det_{\mathrm F}(I + \mathcal R) = L_Q^{-1}\) and \(-\log L_Q = \operatorname{Tr}\log(I+\mathcal R) = \log\det_{\mathrm F}(I+\mathcal R)\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Classical spectral theory of positive trace-class operators. |
| RDQ-012 | Minimal scalar target \(\operatorname{Tr}\mathcal R = 0 \iff \mathcal R = 0 \iff \mathrm{RH}\) is logically equivalent to RH and structurally minimal. | **PROVED EQUIVALENCE** | Non-negativity of eigenvalues \(\delta^2/\gamma^2 \ge 0\). |
| RDQ-013 | Rational pairing kernel \(\kappa_1(z,w) = \frac{4zw}{(z+w)^2} - 1\) satisfies \(\kappa_1(\lambda, \lambda^\#) = \frac{\delta^2}{\gamma^2}\) for \(\lambda^\# = -\bar\lambda\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Exact rational algebraic identity with \((z+z^\#)^2 = -4\gamma^2\) and \(zz^\# = -(\delta^2+\gamma^2)\). |
| RDQ-014 | Relative trace satisfies \(\operatorname{Tr}\mathcal R = \sum_{\lambda\in\Lambda^+} \kappa_1(\lambda, \lambda^\#)\). | **PROVED / EXACT** | Substitution of RDQ-013 into trace definition. |
| RDQ-015 | Divisor-independent arithmetic evaluation of \(\operatorname{Tr}\mathcal R\) or \(D_\zeta(1)\) by isolating \((\lambda, \lambda^\#)\) without constructing the projected divisor is the live open theorem. | **OPEN / LIVE TARGET** | Central research goal (OBL-RDQ-001). |

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |

| TC-DISC-031 | TC Positive-Tail Certification, Surviving Prime Terms, Eventual Positivity Threshold, Lean 4 PSD Theory (263 Theorems), and Conditional Detection Logic Rectification: (1) Cutoff Discrepancy Reproduction & Mechanism: Quadrature truncation at $z = 12$ ($t = 600$) was a finite computational cutoff that captured only the body of the Archimedean kernel, underestimating the full integral. Truncation $t \le 600$ gives $W_{00} \approx 5.286380 \times 10^{11}$, while extension to $z = 320$ ($t \le 16000$) gives $W_{00} \approx 1.032430 \times 10^{12}$ (doubling $W_{00}$ due to the slow Gevrey sub-exponential decay of $\widehat\kappa(z)$ while $z^4 \omega(z/h)$ is still large). Beyond $z = 320$, the tail integral is bounded by $< 130$ ($< 1.3 \times 10^{-10}$ relative error). Truncation at $t=600$ was a finite cutoff, not a full-value enclosure. (2) Full-Sign Certification via PSD Tail Argument: By NIST DLMF 5.7.6, $\frac{d}{dy}\Re\psi(1/4 + iy) = \sum_{n=0}^\infty \frac{2y(n+1/4)}{((n+1/4)^2 + y^2)^2} > 0$ for $y > 0$, so $\omega(t) = \Re\psi(1/4 + it/2) - \log\pi$ is strictly monotonically increasing on $t \ge 0$ and strictly positive ($\omega(t) \ge \omega(10) \approx 0.464 > 0$ for all $t \ge 10$). Because the tail kernel $k_R(v; h) = \frac{1}{\pi} \int_{T}^\infty \omega(t) \|A_h(it)\|^2 \cos(tv) dt$ has positive weight $\omega(t) \ge 0$, the tail matrix $R_T$ is a continuous superposition of rank-1 positive semidefinite matrices $v(t) v(t)^T$, hence $R_T \succeq 0$. Therefore, for any cutoff $T \ge 10$, $\lambda_{\min}(W_{\rm arch}) \ge \lambda_{\min}(M_T) > 0$, rigorously certifying that the complete Archimedean quadratic form on $(-\infty, \infty)$ is strictly positive definite without needing to evaluate tail entries. (3) Surviving Prime Matrix and Asymptotic Ratio: Bounded surviving prime terms via scaling identity $\|\psi_h\|_2^2 = h^{-5}\|\kappa''\|_2^2 + \frac{1}{2}h^{-3}\|\kappa'\|_2^2 + \frac{1}{16}h^{-1}\|\kappa\|_2^2 \le C_\psi h^{-5}$. Exact same-grade resonances survive for all $h > 0$ whenever station pairs have rational ratio matching a prime power ($q = x_\alpha / x_\beta = p^r$), demonstrated on mandatory control window $[7, 17]$ where stations 8 and 16 resonate at $q=2$ with $C_h(0) = \|\psi_h\|_2^2 > 0$. Prime contribution is bounded in operator norm by $\|W_{\rm prime}\|_{\rm op} \le C_{\rm prime} h^{-5}$. However, the ratio $\|W_{\rm prime}\|_{\rm op} / W_{\rm arch} \sim \frac{C_{\rm prime}}{\log(1/h) c_\kappa D_C} \to 0$ as $h \to 0^+$ because the Archimedean diagonal grows as $h^{-5}\log(1/h)$ while prime terms grow at most as $h^{-5}$, proving asymptotic Archimedean dominance. (4) Eventual Positivity Theorem and Arbitrary Complex Coefficients: Computed explicit threshold $h_{\rm pos}(C) > 0$ below which $W(C, h) = W_{\rm arch} - W_{\rm prime} \succ 0$. Connes-Consani (2020) Prop C.1 constructs a global oscillatory test, while localized small-bandwidth bump schemes in $F_{\rm pos}$ fail to detect off-line zeros by Sobolev $H^1$ continuity bounds ($|B(g, g) - B_{\rm crit}(g, g)| \le C \delta \|g\|_{H^1}^2 \ll B_{\rm crit}(g, g)$ as $h \to 0$). | ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |

| TC-DISC-034 | TC Analytic vs Arithmetic Grade Actions, Intertwining Constraint, and Commutator Defect: (1) Fundamental Distinction Established: Analytic TC dilation \(A_K[\zeta](s) = \zeta(\tau^{-K}s)\) acts on Dirichlet bases as \(n \mapsto n^{\tau^{-K}}\) (log-space dilation \(x \mapsto \tau^{-K}x\)); arithmetic grid dilation \(D_K[\zeta](s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s)\) acts on Dirichlet bases as \(n \mapsto \tau^Kn\) (log-space translation \(x \mapsto x + K\log\tau\)). Implicit identification is classified as `CONFLATED_ANALYTIC_AND_GRID_DILATION`. (2) Non-Commutation and Semidirect Group Structure: \(A_K\) and \(D_J\) generate a semidirect product \(\mathbb R \rtimes \tau^{\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}} \subset \operatorname{Aff}_+(\mathbb R)\) satisfying \(A_K D_J = D_{J\tau^{-K}} A_K\). In log-space, translation \(S_K(x) = x + K\log\tau\) and dilation \(T_J(x) = \tau^{-J}x\) satisfy the affine commutator \([S_K, T_J](x) = S_K T_J(x) - T_J S_K(x) = (1 - \tau^{-J})K\log\tau\). (3) Euler Product Duality: Analytic dilation preserves the Euler product via generalized prime bases \(\{p^{\tau^{-K}}\}\); arithmetic grid dilation breaks Euler product multiplicativity because the grid \(L_K = \tau^K \mathbb Z\) is an additive lattice, not a multiplicative semigroup (\(\prod_p (1 - (\tau^Kp)^{-s})^{-1} = \sum \tau^{-K\Omega(n)s}n^{-s} \ne \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s)\)). (4) Mellin Transform Duality: Linear scaling in \(x\)-space maps under Mellin transform to arithmetic grid dilation \(D_K\); power scaling in \(x\)-space maps under Mellin transform to analytic dilation \(A_K\). Case C holds: no universal linear intertwiner across all functions intertwines translation and dilation. (5) Completed \(\xi\) Symmetry: Analytic dilation \(A_K[\xi]\) preserves reflection symmetry about \(\Re(s) = \tau^K/2\); arithmetic grid dilation \(D_K[\xi]\) breaks reflection symmetry by factor \(\tau^{K(2s-1)}\), whose off-critical modulus is \(\tau^{2K\delta}\). (6) Commutator Zero-Evaluation Identity: The normalized multiplier ratio \(R_{\mathrm{norm}}(s) = \tau^{J(\tau^{-K}-1)s}\) evaluated along the zero worldline \(s = \tau^K\rho\) has modulus \(\tau^{K_{\mathrm{eff}}\delta}\) and reflection defect \(B_\rho(K_{\mathrm{eff}}) = 4\sinh^2(K_{\mathrm{eff}}\delta\log\tau/2)\) with \(K_{\mathrm{eff}} = J(\tau^K-1)\), vanishing if and only if \(\delta=0\). (7) Earliest Remaining Implication: Construct a test function \(\psi_K\) or integral kernel such that the arithmetic discrepancy functional \(\Delta_K[\psi] = \langle \psi, \mu_K^{\mathrm{analytic}} - \mu_K^{\mathrm{grid}} \rangle\) has the positive reflection-symmetric spectral expansion \(\sum_{\rho \in Z^+} w_\rho B_\rho(K)\) with \(w_\rho > 0\). | **ACTIONS DISTINGUISHED; CONFLATION DEFECT CORRECTED; SEMIDIRECT AFFINE COMMUTATION LAW PROVED; EULER PRODUCT BREAKAGE ON LATTICE PROVED; MELLIN DUALITY (CASE C) DERIVED; COMMUTATOR ZERO-EVALUATION DEFECT IDENTIFIED; TC BRIDGE STRICTLY OPEN** | Symbolically derived and verified in `tests/test_tc_analytic_vs_arithmetic_intertwining.py` (12 passing tests), `docs/reviews/TC_ANALYTIC_ARITHMETIC_INTERTWINING.md`, `data/tc_analytic_arithmetic_intertwining.json`, `TRANSCENDENTAL_CONTINUATION.md` (Section 23), and `MATH_CONTRACT.md` (Section 9.4). |

(2) Grade Units and Unique Factorization: Unit group is \(U(\mathcal M_\tau) = \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \times \{\pm 1\}\). Every grade defines an invertible grade unit \(u_K = (K, 1)\) with \(u_K \star u_J = u_{K+J}\) and \(u_K^{-1} = u_{-K}\). Every element factors uniquely as \(u_K \star (0, \operatorname{sgn} n) \star \prod_p (0, p)^{v_p(|n|)}\). TC grades do NOT create new primes; \(\tau^K p\) is the native prime \((0, p)\) shifted by unit \(u_K\). (3) Quotient Recovers Native Integer Arithmetic: \(\mathcal M_\tau / U_{\mathrm{grade}} \cong \mathbb Z_{\ne 0}\) via projection \(\pi(K, n) = n\). Formalizes "distinct grids, same arithmetic referent": each coset consists of all grade representations of one integer referent. (4) Refined Euler Product Interpretation: Fixed-grade Dirichlet series \(D_K[\zeta](s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s) = \chi_s(u_K)\prod_p (1 - p^{-s})^{-1}\) is a global grade-unit twist of the universal native Euler product, NOT an independent Euler product. (5) Rational-Grade Escape Theorem: For all \(K \in \mathbb Q \setminus \{0\}\) and \(J \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R} \setminus \{0\}\), \(J\tau^{-K} \notin \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) by Lindemann (1882) transcendence of \(\pi\). Analytic dilation induces \((J, n) \mapsto (J\tau^{-K}, n^{\tau^{-K}})\), simultaneously escaping both \(\mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) and \(\mathbb Z\) (elementary nonintegrality witness: \(1 < 2^{\tau^{-K}} < 2\) for \(K > 0\); general transcendence status is OPEN; counter-control \(\alpha = \log_2 3\) is transcendental yet \(2^\alpha = 3 \in \mathbb Z\)). (6) Governing Decision Point (Fork B Resolved): Algebraic-grade closure is an imposed indexing convention, not an intrinsic requirement of \(\zeta\). The escape \(J\tau^{-K} \notin \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\) holds unconditionally and identically whether RH is true or false (\(\delta\)-independent). Analytic dilation naturally escapes into the ambient completion \(\mathcal M_\tau^{\mathrm{ambient}} = \Gamma_\tau \times \exp(\Lambda_\tau) \subset \mathbb R \times \mathbb R_{>0}\) with no contradiction and no required closure. (7) Origin of Spectral Detector: The bilateral reflection defect of the normalized grade-unit character satisfies \(|\widehat\chi_\rho(u_K)| + |\widehat\chi_{1-\rho}(u_K)| - 2 = \tau^{K\delta} + \tau^{-K\delta} - 2 = 4\sinh^2(K\delta\log\tau/2) = B_\rho(K)\), strictly positive for \(\delta \ne 0\) and vanishing iff \(\delta = 0\). (8) Generic-Base Control: Algebraic base \(b=2\) preserves algebraic closure (\(J b^{-K} \in \mathbb A_{\mathbb R}\)); grade escape is specific to transcendental bases; \(B_\rho(K; b)\) is universal. (2) TC Unitarity Reformulation of RH: A character on \(\mathbb Z\) is unitary if \(|\eta(K)| = 1\) for all \(K \in \mathbb Z\). Proved: \(\eta_\rho \text{ unitary on } \mathbb Z \iff \delta = 0\). Consequently, \(\text{RH} \iff \forall \rho \in \mathcal Z(\zeta), \; \eta_\rho \text{ is unitary on } \mathbb Z\). (3) Representation Meaning of \(B_\rho(K)\): The spectral detector \(B_\rho(K) = |\eta_\rho(K)| + |\eta_\rho(K)|^{-1} - 2 = 4\sinh^2(K\delta\log\tau/2)\) is the exact non-unitarity defect of the grade character, vanishing if and only if \(\eta_\rho\) is unitary (\(\delta = 0\)). (4) Mellin Dilation Unitarity & Origin of \(1/2\) Centering: Multiplicative dilation \(x \mapsto \tau^K x\) is strictly unitary on \(L^2(\mathbb R_{>0}, dx/x)\). On \(L^2(\mathbb R_{>0}, dx)\), normalized dilation \((U_K f)(x) = \tau^{K/2} f(\tau^K x)\) is unitary with Mellin multiplier \(\mathcal M[U_K f](s) = \tau^{-K(s - 1/2)} \mathcal M[f](s) = \eta_s(K) \mathcal M[f](s)\). The \(1/2\) centering is derived from the Lebesgue-measure Mellin-Plancherel isometry line \(\Re(s) = 1/2\). (5) Dual = Adjoint Equivalence: Inversion equals complex conjugation on \(\mathbb Z\) if and only if \(\delta = 0\) (\(\eta_\rho(K)^{-1} = \overline{\eta_\rho(K)} \iff \delta = 0\)). (6) Herglotz Analysis & Weil Equivalence: By Herglotz's Theorem (1911), positive-definite sequences on \(\mathbb Z\) admit only unitary characters in their spectral measure on \(\mathbb T\). Off-line zero characters have \(|q_\rho| = \tau^{-\delta} \ne 1\) and cannot appear in any positive Herglotz measure. Connecting arithmetic grade correlations to a pure zero spectrum is classified as `EQUIVALENT_REFORMULATION_OF_WEIL`. # 61. Faithful Tau-Graded Prime Algebra and Cross-Grade Constraint (TASK-TC-022)

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-DISC-041 | Faithful Tau-Graded Laurent Ring and Grade-Zero Algebraicity Selection: (1) Laurent Ring Isomorphism: Integer-grade arithmetic realizations form the ring \(R_\tau = \mathbb Z[\tau, \tau^{-1}] \subset \mathbb R\) with \(\tau = 2\pi\). Evaluation \(\operatorname{ev}_\tau : \mathbb Z[X, X^{-1}] \to R_\tau\) is an injective ring isomorphism by Lindemann (1882) transcendence of \(\tau\) (\(R_\tau \cong \mathbb Z[X, X^{-1}]\)). (2) Faithful Direct Sum Grading: \(R_\tau = \bigoplus_{K \in \mathbb Z} \mathbb Z \tau^K\). For any finite distinct integer grades \(\{K_1, \dots, K_r\} \subset \mathbb Z\) and algebraic coefficients \(a_j \in \overline{\mathbb Q}\), \(\sum_{j=1}^r a_j \tau^{K_j} = 0 \implies a_1 = \dots = a_r = 0\) (`FINITE_CROSS_GRADE_LINEAR_INDEPENDENCE`). Distinct grades never cancel linearly over \(\overline{\mathbb Q}\). (3) Grade-Zero Algebraicity Selection: A finite Laurent expression \(F(X) \in \overline{\mathbb Q}[X, X^{-1}]\) evaluates to an algebraic native value \(F(\tau) \in \overline{\mathbb Q}\) if and only if all nonzero-grade components vanish identically: \(F(\tau) = a_0 \in \overline{\mathbb Q}\) (`GRADE_ZERO_ALGEBRAICITY_SELECTION`). Laurent decompositions are strictly unique. (4) Units and Graded Primes: \(U(R_\tau) = \{\pm \tau^K : K \in \mathbb Z\}\). For rational prime \(p \in \mathbb P\), \(R_\tau / (p) \cong \mathbb F_p[X, X^{-1}]\) is an integral domain; thus \(p\) is a prime element of \(R_\tau\). The element \(p\tau^K\) is associate to \(p\) via unit \(\tau^K\) and is therefore a prime element of \(R_\tau\). Prime systems \(P_K = \{p\tau^K : p \in \mathbb P\}\) are disjoint sets in \(\mathbb R\) representing the same prime associate classes. (5) No Algebraic Transfer Station: For \(x \in L_K\) and \(y \in L_J\) (\(K \ne J\)), no algebraic scalar \(\alpha \in \overline{\mathbb Q}^\times\) satisfies \(\alpha x = y\) (`NO_ALGEBRAIC_TRANSFER_STATION`). Transfer is achieved solely via the transcendental grade unit \(\tau^{J-K}\). (6) Multiplicative Grade Conservation: Grades add under multiplication (\(\deg(xy) = \deg x + \deg y\)). Opposite grades cancel to grade zero: \((p\tau^K)(q\tau^{-K}) = pq \in \mathbb Z = R_0\). Transcendence cancels multiplicatively. Monomial selection rule: \(M = a \prod_{j=1}^r (p_j \tau^{K_j})^{e_j} \in \overline{\mathbb Q} \iff \sum_{j=1}^r e_j K_j = 0\) (`TOTAL_GRADE_ZERO_ALGEBRAICITY`). | **LAURENT RING ISOMORPHISM PROVED; FAITHFUL DIRECT SUM GRADING PROVED; GRADE-ZERO SELECTION RULE DERIVED; GRADED PRIMES CLASSIFIED AS ASSOCIATES; MULTIPLICATIVE GRADE CONSERVATION FORMALIZED; 16 UNIT TESTS PASSING** | Derived in `docs/reviews/TC_FAITHFUL_GRADED_PRIME_ALGEBRA.md`, formalized in Lean 4 (`formal/RiemannScope/FaithfulGradedAlgebra.lean`), verified in `tests/test_tc_faithful_graded_prime_algebra.py` (16 passing tests), documented in `data/tc_faithful_graded_prime_algebra.json`, `TRANSCENDENTAL_CONTINUATION.md` (Section 29), and `MATH_CONTRACT.md` (Section 9.10). |

Because \(\tau^{-Ks} \ne 0\), \(Z_K^{\mathrm{grid}}(s) = 0 \iff \zeta(s) = 0\). Every arithmetic grade has the exact same zero set: zeros DO NOT move under arithmetic grading. (2) Refined Euler Product: \(Z_K^{\mathrm{grid}}(s) = \tau^{-Ks} \prod_p (1 - p^{-s})^{-1}\). Factoring each prime independently as \((p\tau^K)^{-s}\) would yield \(\tau^{-K\Omega(n)s}\), failing grade conservation for composite numbers. Composite numbers carry a single global grade unit: \(n\tau^K = \tau^K \prod_p p^{v_p(n)}\). (3) Canonical Foundational Status: \(Z_K^{\mathrm{grid}}(s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s)\) is the canonical arithmetic TC zeta family (`ARITHMETIC_TC_IS_GRID_UNIT_TWIST`). The function \(Z_K^{\mathrm{pull}}(s) = \zeta(\tau^{-K}s)\) is reclassified as an auxiliary analytic coordinate dilation. Zero character \(\eta_\rho(K) = \tau^{-K(\rho-1/2)}\) represents the normalized grade-unit twist factor of \(Z_K^{\mathrm{grid}}\) evaluated at spectral parameter \(\rho\). (4) Cross-Grade Constraint Audit: All candidate zeta identities audited: Euler product ratios, logarithmic derivative (coefficient contains transcendental \(\log\tau\)), Riemann converter covariance (conformal change of variables). Bilateral products \((p\tau^K)(p\tau^{-K}) = p^2\) and \(|\eta_\rho(K)| \cdot |\eta_\rho(K)|^{-1} = 1\) balance total grade to zero identically. Bilateral sum \(B_\rho(K) = \tau^{K\delta} + \tau^{-K\delta} - 2\) is non-zero off-line but no zeta identity equates it to an algebraic number. (5) Principal Classifications: Foundational: `ARITHMETIC_TC_IS_GRID_UNIT_TWIST`; Constraint: `NO_CURRENT_ZETA_CROSS_GRADE_BRIDGE_AND_GRADE_CANCELLATION_TRIVIAL`. | **GRID ZETA FAMILY DERIVED; ZERO SET INVARIANCE PROVED; REFINED EULER PRODUCT CONSERVES GRADE; CANONICAL FOUNDATIONAL RECLASSIFICATION COMPLETED; CROSS-GRADE ZETA BRIDGE AUDITED; LEAN 4 FORMALIZATION COMPILED** | Formalized in Lean 4 (`formal/RiemannScope/FaithfulGradedAlgebra.lean`), verified in `tests/test_tc_faithful_graded_prime_algebra.py` (16 passing tests), documented in `docs/reviews/TC_FAITHFUL_GRADED_PRIME_ALGEBRA.md`, `data/tc_faithful_graded_prime_algebra.json`, `TRANSCENDENTAL_CONTINUATION.md` (Section 29), and `MATH_CONTRACT.md` (Section 9.10). |

# 63. Intrinsic Grade-Fiber Arithmetic, Ambient Realization, and the Four-Strata TC Foundation (TASK-TC-025)

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-DISC-046 | Intrinsic Grade-Fiber Arithmetic, Ambient Realization, and the Four-Strata TC Foundation: (1) Tagged Grade Fibers: Canonical grade arithmetic is defined on tagged fibers \(\mathcal{F}_K = \{K\} \times \mathbb{Z} = \{(K, n) : n \in \mathbb{Z}\}\), distinct from real-coordinate subsets \(L_K = \tau^K \mathbb{Z} \subset \mathbb{R}\). Internal operations \((K, m) \oplus_K (K, n) = (K, m + n)\) and \((K, m) \odot_K (K, n) = (K, mn)\) with unit \(1_K = (K, 1)\) make \(\mathcal{F}_K\) canonically isomorphic to \(\mathbb{Z}\) (`INTRINSIC_TC_IS_TRANSPORT_OF_STRUCTURE_ONLY`). Realized multiplication is \((m\tau^K) \odot_K (n\tau^K) = mn\tau^K = ((m\tau^K)(n\tau^K))/\tau^K\). (2) Base Independence: The isomorphism \(\mathcal{F}_K \cong \mathbb{Z}\) holds for any real base \(b > 0\) (`GENERIC_BASE_FIBER_INVARIANCE`), proving that internal arithmetic carries no intrinsic \(\tau\)-dependence. (3) Functorial Transfer & Metrics: Transfer maps \(T_{J \leftarrow K}: (K, n) \mapsto (J, n)\) are canonical ring isomorphisms with \(T_{M \leftarrow J} \circ T_{J \leftarrow K} = T_{M \leftarrow K}\) and real scaling \(\Phi_J(T(x)) = \tau^{J - K}\Phi_K(x)\). Intrinsic normalized size \(N_K(K, n) = n\) is multiplicative; metric \(d_K(m\tau^K, n\tau^K) = |m - n|\) is isometric. Arithmetic functions \(\mu_K, \Lambda_K, \pi_K^{\mathrm{int}}\) are strictly grade-invariant. (4) Intrinsic Zeta vs Ambient Dirichlet Series: Intrinsic arithmetic zeta is \(\zeta_K^{\mathrm{int}}(s) = \sum n^{-s} = \zeta(s)\) with Euler product \(\prod_p (1 - p^{-s})^{-1}\). Ambient series is \(Z_K^{\mathrm{amb}}(s) = \tau^{-Ks}\zeta(s)\) (`AMBIENT_COORDINATE_DIRICHLET_SERIES`). The prefactor \(\tau^{-Ks}\) is the dimensional unit realization factor. (5) Full Ambient Group Algebra: Formulated on \(\overline{\mathbb{Q}}[\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}]\) with evaluation \(\operatorname{ev}_\tau: \sum a_j [K_j] \mapsto \sum a_j \tau^{K_j}\). Kernel \(\ker(\operatorname{ev}_\tau)\) represents all finite algebraic cross-grade collapses. Injectivity is proved on \(\mathbb{Z}\) and \(\mathbb{Q}\), but strictly OPEN on \(\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}\) (`OPEN_FINITE_ALGEBRAIC_CROSS_GRADE_COLLAPSE`). (6) Four-Strata TC Architecture: (I) `INTRINSIC_FIBER_ARITHMETIC`, (II) `AMBIENT_REALIZATION`, (III) `AMBIENT_CROSS_GRADE_ALGEBRA`, (IV) `ANALYTIC_PULLBACK`. # 64. Unit-Rescaling No-Go Theorem and Search for Genuine Arithmetic Grade Coupling (TASK-TC-026)

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-DISC-047 | Unit-Rescaling No-Go Theorem and Zero-Divisor Invariance: (1) General Dirichlet Transform Law: For any intrinsic Dirichlet series \(D(s) = \sum_{n=1}^\infty a_n n^{-s}\) with coefficients preserved across grades (\(a_{(K, n)} = a_n\)), the ambient-coordinate series factors as \(D_K^{\mathrm{amb}}(s) = \sum_{n=1}^\infty a_n (n\tau^K)^{-s} = \tau^{-Ks} \sum_{n=1}^\infty a_n n^{-s} = \tau^{-Ks} D(s)\). Holds on all finite partial sums, throughout the convergence half-plane, and across the domain of meromorphic continuation. (2) Zero-Divisor Preservation: Because \(\tau^{-Ks} = \exp(-Ks\log\tau) \ne 0\) for all \(s \in \mathbb{C}\), multiplication by \(\tau^{-Ks}\) preserves zero locations and multiplicities identically: \(\operatorname{Div}_0(D_K^{\mathrm{amb}}) = \operatorname{Div}_0(D)\), and \(\operatorname{mult}_\rho(D_K^{\mathrm{amb}}) = \operatorname{mult}_\rho(D)\). Pure unit rescaling cannot move, create, or destroy any zero of \(\zeta(s)\), nor can it force the Riemann Hypothesis. (3) Mellin & Log-Translation Forms: For Radon measures under dilation \(x \mapsto \tau^K x\), \(\mathcal{M}[\mu_K](s) = \tau^{K(s-1)}\mathcal{M}[\mu](s)\); for functions \(f_K(x) = f(x/\tau^K)\), \(\mathcal{M}[f_K](s) = \tau^{Ks}\mathcal{M}[f](s)\). In log coordinates \(u = \log x\), translation \(u \mapsto u + K\log\tau\) shifts the Fourier/Laplace transform by \(\tau^{-Ks}\). (4) Logarithmic Derivative Invariance: \(-(D_K^{\mathrm{amb}})'/D_K^{\mathrm{amb}}(s) = K\log\tau - D'/D(s)\). The additive shift \(K\log\tau\) is an entire constant function; poles and residues at all zeros \(\rho\) are strictly preserved: \(\operatorname{Res}_{s=\rho}[-(D_K^{\mathrm{amb}})'/D_K^{\mathrm{amb}}] = -\operatorname{mult}_\rho(D)\). (5) Generic-Base Control: For any real base \(b > 0\), \(D_{b,K}^{\mathrm{amb}}(s) = b^{-Ks}D(s)\). The unit-rescaling no-go theorem is base-independent; transcendence of \(\tau\) plays no role in one-grade transform factorization. (6) Exhaustive Construction Audit: All standard TC constructions audited are classified as either `INTRINSIC_CONSTANT` (\(\zeta_K^{\mathrm{int}}\), Euler product, normalized converter, unit-normalized germ) or `UNIT_RESCALING_FACTORABLE` / `DERIVED_GAUGE_COVARIANCE` (\(Z_K^{\mathrm{amb}}\), \(\Xi_K^{\mathrm{amb}}\), functional equation, ambient converter, log derivative shift). (7) Genuine Coupling & Kernel Audits: No standard construction possesses genuine arithmetic grade coupling (\(a_{n,K} = a_n\) identically; \(a_{n,K}/a_{n,J} = 1\)). No standard zeta identity produces a linear relation in \(\ker(\operatorname{ev}_\tau)\) with algebraic coefficients (`NO_KERNEL_ELEMENT_FROM_STANDARD_ZETA_STRUCTURES_FOUND`). # 66. Ambient Realization Kernel, Algebraic-Power Independence, and Zeta-Bridge Firewall (TASK-TC-028 / TASK-TC-028R)

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-DISC-049 | Ambient Realization Kernel, Rational Support Rank, and Algebraic-Power Independence: (1) Exact Kernel & Support Translation: The ambient realization map $\operatorname{ev}_\tau: \mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}[\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}] \to \mathbb{R}$ (or $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}[\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}] \to \mathbb{C}$) has kernel $\sum a_j (2\pi)^{K_j} = 0$. By support translation invariance, $\sum a_j \tau^{K_j} = 0 \iff \sum a_j \tau^{K_j - K_0} = 0$, establishing that evaluation vanishing is an affine invariant depending solely on grade differences. (2) Rational Support Rank $r(S)$: Defined as $\dim_{\mathbb{Q}} \operatorname{span}_{\mathbb{Q}} \{K_j - K_0\}$; proved base-grade invariant, with exact number-field implementation via `to_number_field`. (3) Rank-Zero Classification: If $r(S) = 0$, evaluation vanishes iff $\sum a_j = 0$ (`RANK_ZERO_TRIVIAL_COEFFICIENT_CANCELLATION`), a purely single-grade coefficient property. (4) Exact Rank-One Dichotomy: For $r(S) = 1$ with direction $\alpha \in \mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}} \setminus \{0\}$, clearing denominators yields a polynomial $P(X) = 0$ for $X = \tau^{\alpha/D}$. Proved that $\operatorname{ev}_\tau$ is injective on $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}[\mathbb{Q}\alpha] \iff \alpha \notin S_\tau$. If $\alpha \in S_\tau$, an explicit non-zero kernel witness is $[\alpha] - A[0]$ with $A = \tau^\alpha \in \overline{\mathbb{Q}}$ (`RANK_ONE_KERNEL_CLASSIFIED`). By external Gelfond–Schneider, $\dim_{\mathbb{Q}} S_\tau \le 1$, so at most one rational line in $\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}$ can contain non-trivial kernel elements (`ONE_EXCEPTIONAL_Q_DIRECTION_ONLY`). Base grade $1 \in \mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{R}}$ is algebraic; $\tau^1 = 2\pi$ is transcendental ($1 \notin S_\tau$). (5) Higher-Rank Reduction ($r \ge 2$): Finite support kernel relations of rational rank $r$ reduce precisely to multivariate algebraic-dependence relations among $r$ algebraic powers of $2\pi$ (`FINITE_KERNEL_REDUCES_TO_ALGEBRAIC_INDEPENDENCE`). Injectivity on $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}[\mathbb{Q}\alpha_1 \oplus \cdots \oplus \mathbb{Q}\alpha_r]$ is strictly equivalent to algebraic independence of $\{\tau^{\alpha_1}, \dots, \tau^{\alpha_r}\}$ over $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$. (6) Strengthened Schanuel Theorem: Rigorous two-case derivation (Case A: $1, \alpha_1, \dots, \alpha_r$ $\mathbb{Q}$-independent; Case B: $1 \in \operatorname{span}_{\mathbb{Q}}\{\alpha_1, \dots, \alpha_r\}$) proves $\operatorname{trdeg}_{\overline{\mathbb{Q}}} \overline{\mathbb{Q}}(\tau^{\alpha_1}, \dots, \tau^{\alpha_r}) = r$. Consequently, Schanuel's conjecture implies full finite-rank injectivity of $\operatorname{ev}_\tau$ on every finite algebraic-grade support (`SCHANUEL_IMPLIES_FULL_FINITE_RANK_INJECTIVITY`). (7) Minimal Open Case: Concretely identified as $P(\tau^{\sqrt{2}}, \tau^{\sqrt{3}}) = 0$ ($r=2$). (8) Bounded Exclusions: PSLQ search on $(\tau^{\sqrt{2}}, \tau^{\sqrt{3}})$ with degree $\le 2$, height $\le 1000$ finds no relation; Arb ball arithmetic with native algebraic enclosure (`expr_to_arb`) and interval lower bounds (`abs_lower()`) certifies absence of relations for 15,624 quadratic polynomials (height $\le 2$, certified distance $> 0.201733$) (`CERTIFIED_FINITE_RELATION_EXCLUSION`). (9) Zeta-Bridge Firewall Audit: All audited zeta quantities ($\gamma_n, \rho_n, \zeta(2n+1), \Gamma(\rho)$ with status `ALGEBRAICITY_UNPROVED`; $\log p, \zeta(2n)$ with status `PROVED_TRANSCENDENTAL`) fail the algebraic-coefficient firewall (`NO_ZETA_TO_KERNEL_BRIDGE_FOUND`). This is strictly an audit finding, not a universal impossibility theorem. Zero-arithmetic research is logically distinct from the TC ambient kernel. (10) Lean Scope Delineation: `LEAN_PROVED: algebraic skeleton / low arity` vs `PROVED_PAPER_DERIVATION: full rank-one and higher-rank classification`. Principal Classification: `FINITE_KERNEL_REDUCES_TO_ALGEBRAIC_INDEPENDENCE`. | **AMBIENT EVALUATION KERNEL REDUCED TO ALGEBRAIC INDEPENDENCE; SUPPORT TRANSLATION INVARIANCE & EXACT RATIONAL RANK PROVED; RANK ZERO & ONE COMPLETELY CLASSIFIED; FULL CONDITIONAL INJECTIVITY UNDER SCHANUEL PROVED; MINIMAL OPEN CASE IDENTIFIED; ARB BALL CERTIFIED EXCLUSIONS PROVED; ZETA-BRIDGE FIREWALL AUDITED; LEAN 4 FORMALIZATION COMPILED; 25 UNIT TESTS PASSING** | Derived in `docs/reviews/TC_AMBIENT_KERNEL_ALGEBRAIC_INDEPENDENCE.md`, formalized in Lean 4 (`formal/RiemannScope/AmbientKernel.lean`), verified in `tests/test_tc_ambient_kernel_algebraic_independence.py` (25 passing tests), documented in `data/tc_ambient_kernel_algebraic_independence.json`, `docs/TC_CURRENT_CANONICAL_STATUS.md`, `TRANSCENDENTAL_CONTINUATION.md`, and `MATH_CONTRACT.md`. |

Integer dilation orbit $f(n) = a_0 + a_1 X^n + a_2 Y^n$ is $F(nL)$ on $L\mathbb{Z}$. Terminology: `EXPONENTIAL_POLYNOMIAL`, `LOG_TAU_EVALUATION`, `INTEGER_DILATION_ORBIT`. | TC-DISC-056 | Three-Grade Hankel Constraint and Transport–Moment Compatibility Audit: (1) Arithmetic Rank-One vs Positive-Moment Spectral Architecture: Investigated the nonzero equal-grade arithmetic energy $\mathcal{M}_K = \langle \mu_K \otimes \mu_K, F_K \rangle > 0$ on compact window $[8, 20]$ across $K \in \{-1, 0, 1\}$. Tested whether exact arithmetic rank one ($\mathcal{H}_0 = \mathcal{M}_{-1}\mathcal{M}_1 - \mathcal{M}_0^2 = 0$) can be reconciled with a spectral positive-moment law ($\mathcal{M}_K = \sum_\rho w_\rho r_\rho^K, w_\rho > 0, r_\rho > 0$) that would force $\mathcal{H}_0 > 0$ under off-critical reflection. (2) Full-Transport Coordinate Incompatibility Theorem: Under the two-parameter kernel ansatz $F_K(x, y) = \tau^{aK}F_0(\tau^{-bK}x, \tau^{-bK}y)$, two-variable Mellin scaling satisfies $H_K(s, t) = \tau^{K(a + b(s+t))}H_0(s, t)$. Pairing with prime-dilation pushforward factors $\tau^{-Ks}\tau^{-Kt}$ and normalizing by $\tau^{-aK}$ yields the normalized spectral grade factor $\tau^{(b-1)K(s+t)}$. Full transport $b = 1$ uniquely preserves native integer station labels and forces arithmetic rank one ($\widehat{\mathcal{H}}_0 = 0$), but identically zeroes the spectral exponent: $(1 - 1)K(s+t) = 0 \implies \tau^0 = 1$. All zero-dependent moment variation $\tau^{2K\delta_\rho}$ is identically eliminated, forcing $\widehat{\mathcal{H}}_0 = 0$ spectrally as well. Conversely, any transport $b \ne 1$ that retains spectral grade dependence breaks exact arithmetic rank one due to irregular discrete prime-power stations across grades ($\mathcal{H}_0 \ne 0$ arithmetically). Principal Classification: `TRANSPORT_MOMENT_INCOMPATIBILITY_PROVED`. (3) Firewalls: Proved signed weights allow negative Hankel determinants despite distinct positive support (`SIGNED_SPECTRAL_WEIGHTS_DO_NOT_IMPLY_HANKEL_POSITIVITY`); quadratic double sums cannot be treated as scalar moment sequences (`DOUBLE_SPECTRAL_SUM_NOT_POSITIVE_MOMENT_SEQUENCE`); unconditioned spectral positivity is Weil-equivalent (`HANKEL_POSITIVITY_IS_RH_EQUIVALENT`); exponent cancellation $(1 - 1)K = 0$ holds for any base $q > 1$ (`GENERIC_PUSHFORWARD_COVARIANCE`). On the stationary reflection sector $\sigma = 1-\bar\rho$ ($\bar z_\sigma = -z_\rho$) under $K = r - d, J = r + d$, proved the relative-grade exponent is $\lambda_{\mathrm{relative}} = \bar z_\sigma - z_\rho = -2z_\rho = -2\delta_\rho - 2i\gamma_\rho$ (`sesquilinear_relative_exponent_at_reflection`, `SESQUILINEAR_RELATIVE_GRADE_EXPONENT_IS_MINUS_TWO_Z`). Proved that relative-grade second differentiation at $d = 0$ on the paired reflection quartet yields $8(\log\tau)^2(\delta_\rho^2 - \gamma_\rho^2)$ mixing radial and imaginary parts (`relative_grade_second_derivative_not_radial`, `RELATIVE_GRADE_DIFFERENTIATION_NOT_PURE_RADIAL`), whereas the independent curvature polynomial $P(u,v) = \frac{1}{2}(u-v)^2$ satisfies $P(z_\rho, -\bar z_\rho) + P(\bar z_\rho, -z_\rho) = 8\delta_\rho^2$ (`REFLECTION_QUARTET_RADIAL_IDENTITY_PRESERVED`). (2) Unconditional Infinitude of Distinct Complex Grade Characters & Complex-Root Projector No-Go: Separated $N(T)$ (with multiplicity) from $N_{\mathrm{dist}}(T)$ (distinct zeros) and invoked the unconditional simple critical-line zero density theorem $N_{\mathrm{dist}}(T) \ge N_0^*(T) \ge (0.4058 - o(1))N(T) \gg T\log T$ (Bui, Conrey & Young 2011, *Acta Arith.* 150, 35–64, Theorem 1.1 & Section 2.2, `arXiv:1002.4127v2`; cf. Conrey 1989; Pratt–Robles–Zaharescu–Zeindler 2020, `arXiv:1802.10521v3`) to prove infinitely many distinct complex exponents $\lambda_{\rho_0,\sigma_n} = \delta_{\rho_0} + i(\gamma_{\rho_0} - \gamma_n)$ and infinitely many distinct complex grade characters $q_{\rho_0,\sigma_n} = \tau^{\lambda_{\rho_0,\sigma_n}} \neq 1$ (`INFINITE_DISTINCT_CRITICAL_LINE_ZEROS_PROVED_FROM_SIMPLE_ZERO_DENSITY`, `INFINITE_DISTINCT_COMPLEX_GRADE_CHARACTERS_PROVED`). # 77. Fauzan Hankel Irrationality Architecture Transfer to Zeta-Zero Arguments, Rigorous Newton Saddle Lower-Envelope & Primitive Norm-Polynomial Proof, and $\pi\leftrightarrow\tau$ Arithmetic-Normalization Analysis (TASK-TC-039 / TASK-TC-039R / TASK-TC-039R2 / TASK-TC-039R3 / TASK-TC-039R4)

| ID | Statement | Classification | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| TC-DISC-062 | Fauzan Hankel Irrationality Architecture Transfer to Zeta-Zero Arguments, Rigorous Newton Saddle Lower-Envelope & Primitive Norm-Polynomial Proof, and $\pi\leftrightarrow\tau$ Arithmetic-Normalization Analysis (`SUBGATE-TC-039A` Gates `Z1`–`Z6` Audit, Repair & Steepest-Descent Closure, `TASK-TC-039`, `TASK-TC-039R`, `TASK-TC-039R2`, `TASK-TC-039R3` & `TASK-TC-039R4`): (1) Gate `Z1` & $\pi\leftrightarrow\tau$ Zero-Carrying Normalizations and Rational Gauge Family: Defined $\mathcal{R}_\pi(z) = \frac{\pi^{-z}\xi(2z)}{\Gamma(z+1)} = (2z-1)\pi^{-2z}\zeta(2z)$, $\mathcal{R}_\tau(z) = \frac{(2\tau)^{-z}\xi(2z)}{\Gamma(z+1)} = (2z-1)\tau^{-2z}\zeta(2z)$ ($\tau = 2\pi, \pi = \tau/2$), and the standardized zero-preserving rational gauge family $\mathcal{R}_c(z) := c^z\mathcal{R}_\tau(z) = (2z-1)c^z\tau^{-2z}\zeta(2z)$ for $c \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ with $c_\tau = 1$ ($\mathcal{R}_1 = \mathcal{R}_\tau$) and $c_\pi = 4$ ($\mathcal{R}_4 = \mathcal{R}_\pi = 4^z\mathcal{R}_\tau$) (`RATIONAL_ZERO_PRESERVING_NORMALIZATION_GAUGE_FAMILY_PROVED`); proved entirety on $\mathbb{C}$ with removable singularities $\mathcal{R}_\tau(1/2) = \tau^{-1} = 1/\tau = 1/(2\pi)$, $\mathcal{R}_\pi(1/2) = 1/\pi = 2/\tau = 2\mathcal{R}_\tau(1/2)$, and $\mathcal{R}_c(1/2) = \sqrt{c}/\tau = \sqrt{c}\mathcal{R}_\tau(1/2)$ (`tau_removable_singularity_half_scaling`), proved exact zero-divisor invariance $\mathcal{R}_c(z) = 0 \iff \mathcal{R}_\tau(z) = 0 \iff \mathcal{R}_\pi(z) = 0$ (`pi_tau_zero_function_scaling`, `rational_gauge_zero_divisor_invariant`, `PI_TAU_NORMALIZATIONS_HAVE_IDENTICAL_ZERO_DIVISOR`, `Z1_ARGUMENT_TARGET_REPRESENTATION_PROVED`), and proved Euler's even-zeta rational core $\tau^{-2m}\zeta(2m) = (-1)^{m+1}\frac{B_{2m}}{2(2m)!} \in \mathbb{Q}$ for $m \ge 0$ (at $m=0$, $\zeta(0) = -1/2 < 0$) and $\tau^{-2m}\zeta(2m) \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ strictly for $m \ge 1$ (`tau_even_zeta_rational_core_identity`, `TAU_NORMALIZATION_EXPOSES_EVEN_ZETA_RATIONAL_CORE`), while the $(2m-1)$ factor flips the sign at $m=0$ ($a_0^{(\tau)} = (-1)(-1/2) = +1/2 > 0$) so $a_m^{(\tau)} = \mathcal{R}_\tau(m) = (-1)^{m+1}\frac{(2m-1)B_{2m}}{2(2m)!} \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ for all $m \ge 0$, $a_m^{(c)} = c^m a_m^{(\tau)} \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ ($\forall m \ge 0$), $a_m^{(\pi)} = 4^m a_m^{(\tau)} \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ ($\forall m \ge 0$), and $b_m^{(c)} = c^{m+1}b_m^{(\tau)} \in \mathbb{Q}_{>0}$ ($\forall m \ge 0$, corresponding to $2m+2 \ge 2$) (`pi_tau_integer_sample_scaling`, `moment_pi_tau_scaling`, `RATIONALIZED_XI_RATIONAL_INTEGER_SAMPLING_PROVED`). (2) Gates `Z2` & `Z3` — Target-Zero-Independent Argument Polynomials & Consecutive-Approximant Strict Positivity: Constructed Newton argument polynomials $N_n^{(c)}(z) \in \mathbb{Q}[z]$ (`NEWTON_ARGUMENT_POLYNOMIALIZATION_PROVED`, `fixed_gauge_degree_invariance`), real ordinate norm polynomials $\mathcal{N}_P(Y) \in \mathbb{Q}[Y]$ (`argument_norm_polynomial_real`, `argument_norm_polynomial_nonneg`, `argument_norm_polynomial_degree_bound`), and multipoint Padé numerators $A_n^{(c)}(z) \in \mathbb{Q}[z]$ (`MULTIPOINT_PADE_ARGUMENT_POLYNOMIALIZATION_PROVED`, `pade_interpolation_zero_residual_identity`), all with `candidate_coefficients_depend_on_gamma1 = False` (`Z2_ALGEBRAIC_ARGUMENT_POLYNOMIALITY_PROVED`). Proved that whenever $\Delta^{n+1}a_0^{(c)} \neq 0$, $\gcd(N_n^{(c)}, N_{n+1}^{(c)}) = 1$ and $G_n^{(c)}(Y) = C_n^{(c)}(\mathcal{N}_{N_n^{(c)}}(Y) + \mathcal{N}_{N_{n+1}^{(c)}}(Y)) \in \mathbb{Z}[Y]$ satisfies $G_n^{(c)}(Y) > 0$ for all $Y \in \mathbb{R}$ (`newton_consecutive_common_root_forces_node`, `newton_consecutive_no_common_root_of_nonzero_samples`, `two_approximant_norm_sum_strict_pos`, `Z3_COFINAL_STRUCTURAL_NONVANISHING_PROVED_FOR_TWO_APPROXIMANT_NORM_SUM`). (3) Repaired Carlson-Free Direct Newton Series & Branch-Free Vertical-Line Steepest-Descent Saddle Proof (`TASK-TC-039R3` / `TASK-TC-039R4` & `TASK-TC-040` preflight synchronization): Proved $\sum_{n=0}^\infty (\Delta^n a_0^{(c)})\binom{z}{n} = \mathcal{R}_c(z)$ on all of $\mathbb{C}$ without Carlson's theorem for every rational gauge in the direct Fubini domain $0 < c < \tau^2 = 4\pi^2$ (where $0 < r_{c,k} = \frac{c}{\tau^2 k^2} < 1$ for all $k \ge 1$, including $c_\tau = 1$ and $c_\pi = 4$) via the exact subtracted mode identity $d_n^*(r) = (-1)^{n-1}[(1-r)^{n-1}(1+(2n-1)r)-1]$, uniform bound $|d_n^*(r)| \le \min(1, 2nr)$, absolute Fubini interchange on $\operatorname{Re} z > 1/2$, and open-set analytic continuation (`NEWTON_SERIES_IDENTITY_FOR_RATIONALIZED_XI_PROVED_WITHOUT_CARLSON`). Under the single-valued small-$u$ substitution $w = -1 + u^2$ ($1+w = u^2$) on $\mathbb{H}_+ = \{\operatorname{Re} u > 0\}$ (distinguishing `EXACT_TRANSFORMED_ACTION` $S_{n,c}^{\mathrm{exact}}(u) = -(n+1)\log(1-u^2) - \frac{\sqrt{c(1-u^2)}}{u}$ from `LOCAL_SADDLE_MODEL_PHI_N` $\Phi_n(u) = nu^2 - \frac{\sqrt{c}}{u}$), transformed the Cauchy / Nörlund–Rice representation of $\Delta^n a_0^{(c)}$ into a branch-free integral along the vertical line $u = x_n + iy$ ($x_n = \frac{1}{2}(\frac{\sqrt{c}}{2n})^{1/3}$) passing through the conjugate saddles $u_\pm = (\frac{\sqrt{c}}{2n})^{1/3}e^{\pm i\pi/3} = x_n(1 \pm i\sqrt{3}) \in \mathbb{H}_+$ while $u_0 = -2x_n < 0 \in \mathbb{H}_-$ lies behind the imaginary-axis pole barrier (`vertical_line_saddle_real_concavity_identity`, `vertical_line_saddle_imag_cubic_identity`), proving $\Delta^n a_0^{(c)} = (-1)^{n+1}\frac{2\sqrt{c}}{\sqrt{3\pi}}n^{1/2}\exp(-\kappa(c)n^{1/3})[\cos(\omega(c)n^{1/3}) + O(n^{-1/3})]$ (`FINITE_DIFFERENCE_SADDLE_ASYMPTOTIC_PROVED`) with leading saddle phase $\phi_0(c) = 0$, $\pi^{2/3}$-free decay constant $\kappa(c) = \frac{3}{2^{5/3}}c^{1/3}$ ($\kappa_\pi = \kappa(4) = \frac{3}{2} = 1.5$, $\kappa_\tau = \kappa(1) = \frac{3}{2^{5/3}} \approx 0.9449407874211548$), and phase constant $\omega(c) = \sqrt{3}\kappa(c) = \frac{3\sqrt{3}}{2^{5/3}}c^{1/3}$ (`FIXED_RATIONAL_NORMALIZATION_GAUGE_DOES_NOT_CHANGE_NEWTON_STRETCHED_EXPONENT`). (4) Rigorous Complex Tail Incomplete-Gamma Asymptotic, Two-Step Lower Envelope, & Primitive Integer Norm-Polynomial Cancellation at Gate `Z5`: Derived the upper incomplete Gamma representation $\int_{(n+1/2)^{1/3}}^\infty 3v^{\beta_\pm}\exp(\lambda_\pm v)\,dv = 3(-\lambda_\pm)^{-(\beta_\pm+1)}\Gamma(\beta_\pm+1, -\lambda_\pm(n+1/2)^{1/3})$ ($\lambda_\pm = -\kappa(c) \pm i\omega(c) = 2\kappa(c)e^{\pm 2\pi i/3}$, $-\frac{3}{\lambda_\pm} = \frac{2^{2/3}}{c^{1/3}}e^{\pm i\pi/3}$) for the complex tail $N_n^{(c)}(\alpha_1) = -\sum_{k=n+1}^\infty (\Delta^k a_0^{(c)})\binom{\alpha_1}{k}$, proving that integration by parts shifts the tail phase by $+\pi/3$ relative to the exact increment $d_{n+1}^{(c)}(\alpha_1) = (\Delta^{n+1}a_0^{(c)})\binom{\alpha_1}{n+1}$ ($\cos^2\theta + \cos^2(\theta+\pi/3) = 1 + \frac{1}{4}\cos 2\theta - \frac{\sqrt{3}}{4}\sin 2\theta = \frac{1}{2} + (\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\cos\theta - \frac{1}{2}\sin\theta)^2 \ge \frac{1}{2}$, `two_phase_cosine_square_lower_bound`, `phase_displacement_pi_over_three_sum_sq_lower_bound`), establishing the rigorous two-sided asymptotic enclosure $C_1(c)n^{-3/2}e^{-2\kappa(c)n^{1/3}} \le E_n^{(c)} \le C_2(c)n^{-1/6}e^{-2\kappa(c)n^{1/3}}$ (`NEWTON_TWO_STEP_RAW_LOWER_ENVELOPE_PROVED`). Proved that for the canonical **primitive integer polynomial** $\widetilde{Q}_n^{(c)}(Y) := M_n^{(c)} Q_n^{(c)}(Y) = \frac{L_n^{(c)}}{g_n^{(c)}} Q_n^{(c)}(Y) \in \mathbb{Z}[Y]$ (dividing out all common integer content $g_n^{(c)}$), its integer leading coefficient $\widetilde{q}_{n,2n+2}^{(c)} = M_n^{(c)} q_{n,2n+2}^{(c)} \ge 1$ forces $M_n^{(c)} \ge \frac{1}{q_{n,2n+2}^{(c)}} = \frac{4^{n+1}((n+1)!)^2}{(\Delta^{n+1}a_0^{(c)})^2}$ (`primitive_multiplier_lower_bound_from_leading_coeff`), so on $\Delta^{n+1}a_0^{(c)} \ne 0$ the leading coefficient cancels identically to give $\widetilde{Q}_n^{(c)}(\gamma_1) \ge \frac{1}{2}\prod_{j=0}^n ((2j-\frac{1}{2})^2 + \gamma_1^2) \ge \frac{1}{2}(\frac{1}{4}+\gamma_1^2)(n!)^2 \to +\infty$ (`primitive_two_approximant_norm_cancellation_lower_bound`), and globally for all $n \ge n_0(c)$ (using $n + 1 - C_0 n^{2/3} \le k_n \le n+1$ from cosine phase spacing $\Delta m \asymp m^{2/3}$, giving $2k_n\log k_n = 2n\log n + O(n)$) the saddle upper bound on $|\Delta^{k_n}a_0^{(c)}|$ proves $\log\widetilde{Q}_n^{(c)}(\gamma_1) \ge 2n\log n - O_c(n) \to +\infty$ and $\frac{-\log|\widetilde{Q}_n^{(c)}(\gamma_1)|}{\deg\widetilde{Q}_n^{(c)}} \le -\log n + O_c(1) \to -\infty$, closing the Newton two-approximant route negatively at Gate `Z5` (`PRIMITIVE_NORM_POLYNOMIAL_LOWER_BOUND_PROVED`, `NEWTON_TWO_APPROXIMANT_PRIMITIVE_ROUTE_RIGOROUSLY_FAILS_GATE_Z5`, `NEWTON_TWO_APPROXIMANT_ROUTE_RIGOROUSLY_FAILS_GATE_Z5`, `CLOSED_NEGATIVE_AT_GATE_Z5`). Proved prime-by-prime valuation bounds $2n\log n + O_c(n) \le \frac{5}{2}n\log n + O_c(n) \le \log L_n^{(c)} \le 3n\log n + O_c(n)$ with $v_p(L_n^{(\pi)}) = v_p(L_n^{(\tau)})$ for all odd primes $p \ge 3$ and $v_2(L_n^{(\tau)}) - v_2(L_n^{(\pi)}) = 2n + O(1)$ (`FIXED_RATIONAL_NORMALIZATION_GAUGES_EQUIVALENT_AT_LEADING_DIOPHANTINE_ORDER`). Scoped multipoint Padé to `MULTIPOINT_PADE_GATE_Z5_NEGATIVE_NUMERICAL_EVIDENCE` ($1 \le n \le 25$, `all_n_no_go_claimed = False`). Proved exact Hankel gauge scaling $D_n^{(c)} = c^{n^2}D_n^{(\tau)}$ and $D_n^{(\pi)} = 4^{n^2}D_n^{(\tau)}$ (`hankel_gauge_scaling_exponent`, `HANKEL_DETERMINANT_RATIONAL_GAUGE_SCALING_PROVED`), repaired the $\pi$-free Lambert–Stieltjes S-fraction product $D_n^{(\pi)} = 6^{-n}\prod_{j=1}^{n-1}[(4j-1)(4j+1)^2(4j+3)]^{-(n-j)}$ ($D_1^{(\pi)}=1/6, D_2^{(\pi)}=1/18900, D_1^{(\tau)}=1/24, D_2^{(\tau)}=1/4838400$), and proved via Euler–Maclaurin and Barnes $G$-function asymptotics that $-\log D_n^{(\pi)} = 2n^2\log n + (4\log 2 - 3)n^2 + O(n\log n)$, $-\log D_n^{(\tau)} = 2n^2\log n + (6\log 2 - 3)n^2 + O(n\log n)$, and $-\log D_n^{(c)} = 2n^2\log n + (6\log 2 - 3 - \log c)n^2 + O(n\log n)$. Proved that removing $(2m)!$ from the denominator moves it into factorial sample growth $|\mathcal{B}(m)| = |B_{2m}| = \frac{2(2m)!}{\tau^{2m}}\zeta(2m)$ ($\log|\mathcal{B}(m)| = 2m\log m - 2(1+\log\pi)m + \frac{1}{2}\log m + \log(4\sqrt{\pi}) + O(m^{-1})$, $\log|\mathcal{K}_0(m)| = 2m\log m - 2(1+\log\pi)m + \frac{3}{2}\log m + \log(4\sqrt{\pi}) + O(m^{-1})$, $R_{\mathrm{OGF}} = 0$, `FACTORIAL_COST_MOVES_FROM_DENOMINATOR_TO_SAMPLE_GROWTH_UNDER_REFLECTION`). (3) Gamma-Ratio Tradeoff Family $\mathcal{B}_q(z) := \frac{\mathcal{K}_0(z)}{\Gamma(z+1)^q} = \frac{z(2z-1)\zeta(1-2z)}{\Gamma(z+1)^q}$ ($q \in \mathbb{Z}_{\ge 0}$) & Irregular-Prime-Immune Two-Sided Denominator Theorem: Proved $\mathcal{B}_q(m) = -\frac{(2m-1)B_{2m}}{2(m!)^q}$ (`gamma_ratio_integer_sample_identity`) and proved unconditionally via the irregular-prime-immune witness $m_p = k_p\frac{p-1}{2}$ ($k_p \in \{\lfloor\frac{2n}{p-1}\rfloor, \lfloor\frac{2n}{p-1}\rfloor - 1\}$, $k_p \not\equiv -1 \pmod p$) that $q\log(n!) + \sum_{3\le p\le n}(1 - q\lfloor\log_p n\rfloor)\log p \le \log D_{q,n} \le q\log(n!) + \vartheta(2n+1) + \log 2$, so $\log D_{q,n} = q\,n\log n + O_q(n)$ and $\log|\mathcal{B}_q(m)| = (2-q)m\log m + O_q(m)$ (`PROVED_TWO_SIDED_DENOMINATOR_ASYMPTOTIC`, `GAMMA_RATIO_FACTORIAL_BUDGET_TRADEOFF_PROVED`, `gamma_ratio_budget_conservation_identity`, `gamma_ratio_newton_basis_total_cost_conservation`, `factorial_budget_algebraic_inequality`). (4) Route B — Positive-Moment Bernoulli Stieltjes–Hankel Architecture: Defined $\mathcal{P}(z) := -e^{i\tau z/2}\mathcal{B}(z) = 2ze^{i\pi z}\zeta(1-2z)$ (`sign_adjusted_bernoulli_positive_sample_identity`) with positive Stieltjes moments $\mu_m := \mathcal{P}(m+1) = (-1)^m B_{2m+2} = |B_{2m+2}| = \int_0^\infty y^m\,d\nu_\tau(y)$ ($d\nu_\tau(y) = \frac{\tau\sqrt{y}}{4\sinh^2(\tau\sqrt{y}/2)}\,dy$, $\mu_0=1/6, \mu_1=1/30, \mu_2=1/42$, `BERNOULLI_EVEN_ABSOLUTE_VALUES_STIELTJES_MOMENT_SEQUENCE_PROVED`), proved the exact Stieltjes–Carlitz–Touchard Jacobi recurrence $\alpha_k^{\mathcal{B}} = \frac{(k+1)(2k+1)(4k^2+6k+1)}{(4k+1)(4k+5)}$ and $\beta_k^{\mathcal{B}} = \frac{H_{k+1}^{\mathcal{B}}H_{k-1}^{\mathcal{B}}}{(H_k^{\mathcal{B}})^2} = \frac{k^3(k+1)(2k-1)(2k+1)^3}{(4k-1)(4k+1)^2(4k+3)} > 0$ ($\beta_1^{\mathcal{B}} = 18/175$, `carlitz_touchard_hankel_recurrence_positivity`), and proved $H_n^{\mathcal{B}} = \det(\mu_{i+j})_{0\le i,j<n} = 6^{-n}\prod_{k=1}^{n-1}(\beta_k^{\mathcal{B}})^{n-k} > 0$ ($H_1^{\mathcal{B}}=1/6, H_2^{\mathcal{B}}=1/350, H_3^{\mathcal{B}}=4/56595, H_4^{\mathcal{B}}=128/11022011$) with $\log H_n^{\mathcal{B}} = 2n^2\log n - (3 + 2\log 2)n^2 + O(n\log n) \to +\infty$, while proving that the Stieltjes transform $S_{\mathcal{B}}(w) = \int_0^\infty \frac{d\nu_\tau(y)}{w-y} = 1 + \frac{1}{2\sqrt{-w}} - \sqrt{-w}\psi'(\sqrt{-w}) \sim \sum_{m=0}^\infty \frac{|B_{2m+2}|}{w^{m+1}} = \frac{1}{6w} + \frac{1}{30w^2} + \frac{1}{42w^3} + \cdots$ and monic orthogonal polynomials $P_n^{\mathcal{B}}(X)$ live in the integration variable $X = y = x^2$ rather than the Mellin zero-argument variable $z = \rho/2$ (`BERNOULLI_STIELTJES_HANKEL_POSITIVITY_DOES_NOT_COUPLE_TO_ZETA_ZERO_ARGUMENT`, `primitive_hankel_polynomial_nonzero_schema`). (2) Bounded `TC-042F` Reconciliation (`TC042F_BOUNDED_RECONCILIATION_VERIFIED_ALL_SIX_DEFECTS_FIXED`): Verified and repaired all 6 `TC-042F` discrepancies: (i) complex reflected-pair conjugate-inverse invariance $q(\lambda u, \bar{\lambda}^{-1}v) = q(u, v)$ where $\bar{\lambda}^{-1} = \lambda/|\lambda|^2$ (`complex_reflected_pair_conjugate_inverse_invariance`, falsifying naive $\lambda^{-1}$ at $\lambda = i, u = v = 1$); (ii) two-sided sesquilinear shift-difference identity $\langle(I-S_u)f, (I-S_u)g\rangle_{L^2(d^\times y)} = 2\langle f,g\rangle_{L^2} - \langle S_u f,g\rangle_{L^2} - \langle S_{-u}f,g\rangle_{L^2} = \Phi_{f,g}(1) - \Phi_{f,g}(e^u)$ (`sesquilinear_shift_difference_identity`); (iii) Stieltjes lower integration-by-parts boundary value $E(1) = \psi(1) - 1 = -1$ at $x = 1$ with zero jump of $\psi$ at $x = 1$; (iv) reconciled Attempt B quantities $\|A_\tau^B\psi_\tau\|^2 = +0.5947678335$, $-c_\tau^B\|\psi_\tau\|^2 = -1.6771975212$ ($c_\tau^B = 3.3721834192256654 \pm 3.92 \times 10^{-56}$), $J_0 = +0.0259597613$, $B_{\mathrm{transfer}}(\psi_\tau) = +0.9687652063$, smooth Attempt B part $= -1.0824296878$, and $P_E(\psi_\tau) = +1.0833801656$ vs stale values $-0.399957, +0.400908$; (v) complete 256-bit Arb error enclosures on $[0, 0.25]$ Taylor/Cauchy bound $5.47 \times 10^{-28}$, $[20, \infty)$ analytic tail $7.67 \times 10^{-23}$, and Rosser–Schoenfeld prime-power tail $6.31 \times 10^{-9}$ for $n > 25{,}000$, reducing the spatial-vs-analytic gap from $3.7233 \times 10^{-14}$ to $7.6750 \times 10^{-23}$; (vi) explicit Lean scope table and `two_dim_gram_cauchy_schwarz_from_nonneg`. (3) Admissible Cross-Grade Compatibility Specification on $\mathcal{S}_\tau$ (`TASK-TC-043` / `TASK-TC-043R`): Specified the finite graded theta span $\mathcal{S}_\tau = \{\sum_{j=1}^N c_j T_{K_j}\psi_\tau : K_j \in \overline{\mathbb{Q}}\cap\mathbb{R}\}$, proved complex zero worldline collinearity $\tau^K\rho = \tau^J\rho' \implies \tau^{K-J} = \rho'/\rho \in \mathbb{R}_{>0}$ (`cross_grade_complex_zero_ray_collinearity`), separated rational vs irrational algebraic grade differences, and contrasted Case A vs Case B synthetic quartet controls.
